you wrote it, on this run's storage volume, at the path it already has — it is
not moved, renamed or copied. A later round reads it there, by that absolute
workspace path, so a checkpoint you keep is a checkpoint the next round can

your cwd to GitHub but skips every file of 100 MB or
more, so trained weights and large binary artifacts never leave the volume.

<artifact_info>
id: art_6zBfl3kDjeiP
type: experiment
title: Do algorithm bans pick a substitute or a ladder?
summary: >-
  FRLP output-space screen of claim C1 (do prompt-level algorithm bans on Qwen3-8B pick a specific substitute or move mass
  along a generic ladder?) on the two iteration-1 path-search tasks (T1 grid, 6 algorithms; T2 weighted shortest path, 5).
  Provides: (1) 1,170 forced generations (10 forced paraphrases per algorithm, 3 adaptive vLLM rounds) labelled with the canonical
  runtime-led exp1 labeller (re-check: gold 0.965, references 22/22), name-scrubbed and verified; 211 selected programs (M=20
  per algorithm after the pre-registered cut rule; bidirectional Dijkstra 11) in data/forced_selected.jsonl. (2) Teacher-forced
  scores of every program in 684 scoring units (condition x program set, Q1-Q7 + part of Q8; per-program parquet + per-token
  npz in scores/), computed with bf16 weights but fp32 activations/TF32 matmuls because bf16 numerics moved per-program log-likelihoods
  by up to 2 nats between equivalent paths (logs/scorer_validation*.json). (3) FRLP cells (yield-corrected mixture-proposal
  importance sampling, ESS per cell; results/frlp_cells.jsonl, 6 variants). (4) FRLP gate vs 19 exp1 sampled events: primary
  FAIL on calibration (a) (m=0.83<0.9: scrubbed programs give DFS mass under the T1 baseline), PASS on (b) 0.92 nats and (c)
  16/19; 98% of gate cells have ESS<5. Diagnostic: the UNSCRUBBED variant passes all three (S=0.95, m=1.00; 1.18 nats; 17/19),
  so the failure is a name-scrubbing artifact; single-proposal also passes (reported, not adopted). (5) C1 (descriptive):
  pooled X-specific SHARE 0.55 [0.25, 0.67] (neutral twins), 0.66 [0.49, 0.87] (mention twins) -> pooled branch UNDETERMINED,
  robust across both references and the ESS-weighted variant; T1 is X-SPECIFIC-UNAIMED under the mention reference. Residuals
  are not aimed at the prime-and-veto profile (AIM -0.10, null p95 0.20, p=0.80) or free-form association (p=0.77); the 8
  generic profiles do not form a coherent ladder (mean centred cosine 0.34 T1, -0.02 T2). Unscrubbed W1-only sensitivity:
  SHARE 0.69 [0.51, 0.84] (X-specific). Bans of absent algorithms raise their own FRLP (priming, +2.5 to +27 nats). Iteration-1
  sham shifts (Floyd->BF/SPFA) equal the ladder component. (6) Frozen for the confirmation bench (hash-chained in commitments.log):
  frozen/aim_profiles.json, frozen/ladder_profile.json, frozen/templates_nouns_padding.json, frozen/code_sha256.json; prompts/prompts_v2.json
  (604 conditions, nouns, ' .' padding). Independent re-derivation (audit/rederive.py) matches gate, C1 SHARE, AIM and FRLP
  cells to <1e-15; placebo AIM sits at its null. Not run: Q8 shams (partial), Q9 variant (ii), exp2-exp5 extension. method_out.json
  (exp_gen_sol_out): 19 gate-event examples + 11 C1 rows. Recommendation: the confirmation bench should score unscrubbed programs.

<data_loading_pattern>
`mini_demo_data.json` = curated subset for the demo.
Use this pattern for Colab compatibility (GitHub URL with local fallback):
```python
GITHUB_DATA_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ai-inventor-papers/ai-invention-295f8e-prompt-bans-substitute-not-just-subtract/fork/run_jSassd-YWLVC/round-2/experiment-7/demo/mini_demo_data.json"
import http.client
import json
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

def load_data():
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(GITHUB_DATA_URL, timeout=15) as response:
            return json.loads(response.read().decode())
    # Network errors, timeouts, a placeholder URL, bad JSON, a cut-off response.
    except (OSError, ValueError, http.client.HTTPException) as e:
        print("GitHub data unavailable, using local mini_demo_data.json:", e)
    local = Path("mini_demo_data.json")
    if local.exists(): return json.loads(local.read_text())
    raise FileNotFoundError("Could not load mini_demo_data.json")
```
</data_loading_pattern>

take this repo as inspiration: https://github.com/DrejcPesjak/llm-creativity
use one or more open-weight models (minimum 1B params), probably instruction tuned it
and first run behavioral analysis (similar to repo mentioned above), then move to interpretability (can be manifolds, or also other techniques like sae, circuits, nla, j-lens, logit lens...)
the question is: how does "creativity" manifest inside the model, and can we influence it?
(in layman terms, can we make llms give truly creative and novel solutions?)

(you do not need to implement everything bellow, see whats reasonable, interesting and might lead to new insights)
is "temp" really the "llm creativity knob"?  (Astra found "coincidentally" similar direction for navier-stokes solution, as Buckmaster & Levent)
(always same seed)
1 a) does a tight temp , with topk=10 produce 10 reasoning trajectories of 1 idea/solution but same 10 phrasings
b) or does tight temp, with topk=10 produce 10 reasoning trajectories of 10 ideas/solutions with 1 phrasing each
c) and does loosing up temp, make a) more to 2/3/4 ideas each with 10 phrasings ; or make b) keep original 10 ideas, now each with 10 phrasings
(temp is rather a minor part in this idea, put more focus on the following things)
test: "implement path saerch algorithm" -> does llm always design BFS?
2 can context seeding help? prepand "seed=d32qxk4nt43" or "seed=(horse chair roof love)"
hyp: is llm a probablistic model, and thus gives you the average/mean answer? and only context pushes you into a different subspace
 - ie of possible range of answer [0,100] , on direct question llm gives you 50. then with context you move to [80,100] subspace and llm gives you 90
3 so how much does prompt instructions matter? ("Make a new algorithm", "Make a fully novel algorithm never seen before ... 2000 words ..."
-> analyze the internals , all the runs that produced BFS , vs all runs that produced DFS , vs dijkstra ...
  -> analyze with thought anchors, NLA, SAE ...
  -> what is the average answer? plot a "path search algorithm" manifold, color each algorithm centroid by implemented-count (lets say we have 10 algorithms in total of 100 gens)
     -> then prohibit the top 5 in prompt, and see if the implemented count moves to the edge of those 5, or to the opposite side (if its a circle manifold)
  - (manifold lit: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.05115 , https://www.goodfire.com/research/manifold-steering ) - geodesic and mds
    - (the reports need to contain actual geometry plots, actual activation space, not some summary bar charts)

sum_j exp(L_j) over measurable j and m = exp(L_default)/S;\n    PASS if mean S in [0.3, 3] and mean m >= 0.9.\n(b) per event e and paraphrase k: q_ek(j) ~ exp(L_j(e,k)) over allowed labels (half-1 programs);\n    q' = 0.93 q + 0.05 uniform(allowed) + 0.02 other; reference u = 0.98 uniform(allowed) + 0.02 other;\n    d = mean over the paraphrase's own samples of log q'(label) - log u(label) (banned-label samples excluded);\n    d_e = mean over paraphrases; PASS if mean_e d_e >= 0.3 nats.\n(c) argmax_j mean_k q_ek equals the realised attempted mode in >= 80% of clear-mode events.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport math\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\nfrom loguru import logger\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\n\nfrom config import (DEFAULT_MODE, ESS_FLAG, GATE_A_M_MIN, GATE_A_S_RANGE, GATE_B_MIN, GATE_C_MIN, MODES, OTHER,\n                    RESULTS, TASKS, read_json, setup_logging, utc_now, write_json)\nfrom frlp import VARIANTS, TaskData, build_task_data\n\n\ndef implied(td: TaskData, cond: str, allowed: list[str], half: str, vname: str) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict]:\n    Ls, ess = {}, {}\n    for j in allowed:\n        if j in td.measurable:\n            c = td.cell(cond, j, half, vname)\n            if c[\"n\"] > 0 and np.isfinite(c[\"L\"]):\n                Ls[j] = c[\"L\"]\n                ess[j] = c[\"ess\"]\n    if not Ls:\n        return {}, ess\n    mx = max(Ls.values())\n    z = sum(math.exp(v - mx) for v in Ls.values())\n    return {j: (math.exp(Ls[j] - mx) / z if j in Ls else 0.0) for j in allowed}, ess\n\n\ndef gate(tds: dict[str, TaskData], vname: str = \"primary\", max_para: int = 5) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    ev_file = read_json(RESULTS / \"gate_events.json\")\n    res: dict[str, Any] = {\"variant\": vname, \"events\": [], \"gate_a\": {}}\n    # (a)\n    S_all, m_all = [], []\n    for t in TASKS:\n        td = tds[t]\n        base = next(e for e in ev_file[\"events\"] if e[\"event_id\"] == f\"{t}|baseline\")\n        rows = []\n        for p in base[\"paraphrases\"][:max_para]:\n            cond = f\"exp1|baseline|p{p['paraphrase_id']}\"\n            Ls = {j: td.cell(cond, j, \"all\", vname)[\"L\"] for j in td.measurable}\n            Ls = {j: v for j, v in Ls.items() if np.isfinite(v)}\n            if not Ls:\n                continue\n            S = sum(math.exp(v) for v in Ls.values())\n            m = math.exp(Ls.get(DEFAULT_MODE[t], -math.inf)) / S if S > 0 else math.nan\n            rows.append({\"paraphrase_id\": p[\"paraphrase_id\"], \"S\": S, \"m_default\": m,\n                         \"L\": Ls})\n            S_all.append(S)\n            m_all.append(m)\n        res[\"gate_a\"][t] = rows\n    mS = float(np.mean(S_all)) if S_all else math.nan\n    mm = float(np.mean(m_all)) if m_all else math.nan\n    res[\"a\"] = {\"mean_S\": mS, \"mean_m_default\": mm,\n                \"pass\": bool(GATE_A_S_RANGE[0] <= mS <= GATE_A_S_RANGE[1] and mm >= GATE_A_M_MIN)}\n    # (b) and (c)\n    d_events, hits, n_clear, low_ess, n_cells = [], 0, 0, 0, 0\n    pooled_impl, pooled_real = [], []\n    for e in ev_file[\"events\"]:\n        t = e[\"task\"]\n        td = tds[t]\n        allowed = [j for j in MODES[t] if j not in e[\"banned\"]]\n        per_para, qs = [], []\n        for p in e[\"paraphrases\"][:max_para]:\n            cond = f\"exp1|{e['condition']}|p{p['paraphrase_id']}\"\n            q, ess = implied(td, cond, allowed, \"h1\", vname)\n            for v in ess.values():\n                n_cells += 1\n                low_ess += int(v < ESS_FLAG)\n            if not q:\n                continue\n            qs.append(q)\n            ua = 0.98 / len(allowed)\n            labs = [lbl if lbl in MODES[t] else OTHER for lbl in p[\"labels\"]]\n            excluded = sum(lbl in e[\"banned\"] for lbl in labs)\n            ds = []\n            for lbl in labs:\n                if lbl in e[\"banned\"]:\n                    continue\n                if lbl == OTHER:\n                    ds.append(0.0)\n                else:\n                    qp = 0.93 * q.get(lbl, 0.0) + 0.05 / len(allowed)\n                    ds.append(math.log(qp) - math.log(ua))\n            per_para.append({\"paraphrase_id\": p[\"paraphrase_id\"], \"d\": float(np.mean(ds)) if ds else math.nan,\n                             \"n_scored\": len(ds), \"n_banned_excluded\": excluded, \"q\": q,\n                             \"ess\": ess})\n        if not qs:\n            continue\n        qbar = {j: float(np.mean([q[j] for q in qs])) for j in allowed}\n        d_e = float(np.nanmean([pp[\"d\"] for pp in per_para]))\n        arg = max(qbar, key=qbar.get)\n        real = e[\"distribution_allowed\"]\n        n_real = sum(v for k, v in real.items() if k != OTHER) or 1\n        for j in allowed:\n            pooled_impl.append(qbar[j])\n            pooled_real.append(real.get(j, 0) / n_real)\n        hit = arg == e[\"realised_mode\"]\n        if e[\"clear_mode\"]:\n            n_clear += 1\n            hits += int(hit)\n        d_events.append(d_e)\n        res[\"events\"].append({\"event_id\": e[\"event_id\"], \"d_e\": d_e, \"implied_mean\": qbar, \"argmax\": arg,\n                              \"realised_mode\": e[\"realised_mode\"], \"realised_allowed\": real, \"hit\": hit,\n                              \"clear_mode\": e[\"clear_mode\"], \"paraphrases\": per_para})\n    mean_d = float(np.mean(d_events)) if d_events else math.nan\n    res[\"b\"] = {\"mean_d_e\": mean_d, \"n_events\": len(d_events), \"pass\": bool(mean_d >= GATE_B_MIN)}\n    res[\"c\"] = {\"hits\": hits, \"n_clear\": n_clear, \"rate\": hits / max(1, n_clear),\n                \"pass\": bool(n_clear > 0 and hits / n_clear >= GATE_C_MIN)}\n    res[\"pass\"] = bool(res[\"a\"][\"pass\"] and res[\"b\"][\"pass\"] and res[\"c\"][\"pass\"])\n    rho = spearmanr(pooled_impl, pooled_real).correlation if len(pooled_impl) > 2 else math.nan\n    res[\"pooled_spearman_implied_vs_realised\"] = float(rho) if rho is not None else math.nan\n    res[\"ess_cells\"] = {\"n\": n_cells, \"n_below_5\": low_ess, \"frac_below_5\": low_ess / max(1, n_cells),\n                        \"flag_more_than_half_low\": bool(low_ess > n_cells / 2)}\n    if not res[\"pass\"]:\n        res[\"failure_kind\"] = [k for k in (\"a\", \"b\", \"c\") if not res[k][\"pass\"]]\n    return res\n\n\ndef succession_descriptive(tds: dict[str, TaskData]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Step 7.2 part: FRLP-implied landing under exp1's own step-2 prompts vs exp1's sampled step-2 labels.\"\"\"\n    ev_file = read_json(RESULTS / \"gate_events.json\")\n    out = {}\n    for e in ev_file[\"succession_step2_descriptive\"]:\n        t = e[\"task\"]\n        td = tds[t]\n        allowed = [j for j in MODES[t] if j not in e[\"banned\"]]\n        qs, banned_mass = [], []\n        for p in e[\"paraphrases\"]:\n            cond = f\"exp1|succession:2|p{p['paraphrase_id']}\"\n            q, _ = implied(td, cond, allowed, \"h1\", \"primary\")\n            if q:\n                qs.append(q)\n            Ls = {j: td.cell(cond, j, \"h1\", \"primary\")[\"L\"] for j in td.measurable}\n            Ls = {j: v for j, v in Ls.items() if np.isfinite(v)}\n            if Ls:\n                tot = sum(math.exp(v) for v in Ls.values())\n                banned_mass.append(sum(math.exp(Ls[j]) for j in e[\"banned\"] if j in Ls) / tot)\n        out[e[\"event_id\"]] = {\n            \"implied_landing\": {j: float(np.mean([q[j] for q in qs])) for j in allowed} if qs else None,\n            \"implied_banned_mass\": e[\"n_samples\"])}\n    return out\n\n\n@logger.catch(reraise=True)\ndef main() -> None:\n    setup_logging(\"gate\")\n    tds = build_task_data()\n    tp = read_json(RESULTS.parent / \"logs\" / \"throughput.json\")\n    max_para = int(tp.get(\"gate_paraphrases\", 5))\n    out = {\"utc\": utc_now(), \"primary\": gate(tds, \"primary\", max_para)}\n    out[\"sensitivity\"] = {}\n    for v in    write_json(RESULTS / \"gate.json\", out)\n    p = out[\"primary\"]\n    logger.info(f\"GATE {out['verdict']}: a={p['a']} b={p['b']} c={p['c']} rho={p['pooled_spearman_implied_vs_realised']}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n=== REDERIVE ===\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"T6 independent re-derivation: recompute 10 random FRLP cells, the gate statistics (a, b, c), the C1 point SHARE\n(primary reference, both tasks + pooled) and the AIM point value directly from the raw parquet score files with\nseparate code (no import of frlp.py / gate.py / c1.py), and compare with results/*.json (tolerance 1e-9).\nPlacebo: template labels permuted across ban rows -> the AIM should sit at its null.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport math\nimport random\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\nMODES = {\"t1\": [\"bfs\", \"dfs\", \"bibfs\", \"iddfs\", \"astar_gbfs\", \"dijkstra\"],\n         \"t2\": [\"dijkstra\", \"bf_spfa\", \"bidijkstra\", \"floyd\", \"dial\"]}\nWS = [\"W1\", \"W2\", \"W3\", \"W4\", \"W5\"]\nRAND = [\"A1001\", \"A1002\", \"A1003\", \"B2001\", \"B2002\", \"B2003\"]\n\n\ndef lse(x):\n    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    m = x.max()\n    return float(m + np.log(np.exp(x - m).sum()))\n\n\ndef load():\n    units = [json.loads(l) for l in (ROOT / \"scores\" / \"units_done.jsonl\").read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]\n    seen, recs = set(), []\n    for u in units:\n        if (u[\"task\"], u[\"uid\"]) in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add((u[\"task\"], u[\"uid\"]))\n        df = pd.read_parquet(ROOT / \"scores\" / \"cells\" / u[\"task\"] / f\"{u['uid']}.parquet\")\n        key = u[\"variant\"] + \"|\" + u[\"cond_id\"]\n        for pid, tot in zip(df[\"prog_id\"], df[\"total\"]):\n            recs.append((u[\"task\"], key, pid, float(tot)))\n    S = {}\n    for t, k, p, v in recs:\n        S.setdefault(t, {}).setdefault(k, {})[p] = v\n    return S\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    S = load()\n    sel = [json.loads(l) for l in (ROOT / \"data\" / \"forced_selected.jsonl\").read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]\n    yl = json.loads((ROOT / \"results\" / \"yields.json\").read_text())[\"yields\"]\n    tp = json.loads((ROOT / \"logs\" / \"throughput.json\").read_text())\n    M = int(tp[\"M\"])\n    progs = {t: [r for r in sel if r[\"task\"] == t and r[\"rank\"] < min(r[\"m_j\"], M)] for t in MODES}\n    meas = {t: [j for j in MODES[t] if yl[f\"{t}|{j}\"][\"measurable\"] and any(r[\"j\"] == j for r in progs[t])]\n            for t in MODES}\n    lq = {}\n    for t in MODES:\n        for r in progs[t]:\n            v = [S[t].get(f\"primary|forced|{r['j']}|{r['family']}|k{k}\", {}).get(r[\"prog_id\"]) for k in range(10)]\n            if all(x is not None for x in v):\n                lq[r[\"prog_id\"]] = lse(v) - math.log(10)\n\n    def L(t, cond, j, half=\"all\"):\n        key = cond if \"|\" in cond.split(\"|\")[0] or cond.startswith((\"primary|\", \"edit|\", \"edit2|\")) else cond\n        key = (\"edit\" if cond.startswith(\"edit|\") else \"primary\") + \"|\" + cond\n        ps = [r for r in progs[t] if r[\"j\"] == j and (half == \"all\" or r[\"half\"] == half)]\n        lw = [S[t][key][r[\"prog_id\"]] - lq[r[\"prog_id\"]] for r in ps if key in S[t] and r[\"prog_id\"] in S[t][key]\n              and r[\"prog_id\"] in lq]\n        if not lw:\n            return float(\"nan\")\n        return math.log(yl[f\"{t}|{j}\"][\"yield\"]) + lse(lw) - math.log(len(lw))\n\n    def Lmix(t, condA, condB, j, half=\"all\"):\n        \"\"\"virtual neutral: noun A on h1, noun B on h2\"\"\"\n        ps = [r for r in progs[t] if r[\"j\"] == j and (half == \"all\" or r[\"half\"] == half)]\n        lw = []\n        for r in ps:\n            key = \"primary|\" + (condA if r[\"half\"] == \"h1\" else condB)\n            if key in S[t] and r[\"prog_id\"] in S[t][key] and r[\"prog_id\"] in lq:\n                lw.append(S[t][key][r[\"prog_id\"]] - lq[r[\"prog_id\"]])\n        return math.log(yl[f\"{t}|{j}\"][\"yield\"]) + lse(lw) - math.log(len(lw)) if lw else float(\"nan\")\n\n    out = {\"checks\": {}}\n    # 10 random FRLP cells\n    cells = [json.loads(l) for l in (ROOT / \"results\" / \"frlp_cells.jsonl\").read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]\n    prim = [c for c in cells if c[\"variant\"] == \"primary\" and not c[\"condition\"].startswith(\"edit2\")]\n    random.Random(3).shuffle(prim)\n    diffs = []\n    for c in prim[:10]:\n        v = L(c[\"task\"], c[\"condition\"], c[\"algorithm\"], c[\"half\"])\n        diffs.append(abs(v - c[\"L\"]))\n    out[\"checks\"][\"frlp_cells_max_absdiff\"] = max(diffs)\n    # gate (a)\n    gate = json.loads((ROOT / \"results\" / \"gate.json\").read_text())[\"primary\"]\n    ev = json.loads((ROOT / \"results\" / \"gate_events.json\").read_text())\n    npara = int(tp.get(\"gate_paraphrases\", 5))\n    Ss, ms = [], []\n    for t in MODES:\n        base = next(e for e in ev[\"events\"] if e[\"event_id\"] == f\"{t}|baseline\")\n        for p in base[\"paraphrases\"][:npara]:\n            Ls = {j: L(t, f\"exp1|baseline|p{p['paraphrase_id']}\", j) for j in meas[t]}\n            tot = sum(math.exp(v) for v in Ls.values())\n            Ss.append(tot)\n            ms.append(math.exp(Ls[{\"t1\": \"bfs\", \"t2\": \"dijkstra\"}[t]]) / tot)\n    out[\"checks\"][\"gate_a_S_absdiff\"] = abs(np.mean(Ss) - gate[\"a\"][\"mean_S\"])\n    out[\"checks\"][\"gate_a_m_absdiff\"] = abs(np.mean(ms) - gate[\"a\"][\"mean_m_default\"])\n    # gate (b)/(c)\n    des, hits, ncl = [], 0, 0\n    for e in ev[\"events\"]:\n        t = e[\"task\"]\n        allowed = [j for j in MODES[t] if j not in e[\"banned\"]]\n        qs, ds = [], []\n        for p in e[\"paraphrases\"][:npara]:\n            Ls = {j: L(t, f\"exp1|{e['condition']}|p{p['paraphrase_id']}\", j, \"h1\") for j in allowed if j in meas[t]}\n            mx = max(Ls.values())\n            z = sum(math.exp(v - mx) for v in Ls.values())\n            q = {j: (math.exp(Ls[j] - mx) / z if j in Ls else 0.0) for j in allowed}\n            qs.append(q)\n            vals = []\n            for lbl in p[\"labels\"]:\n                lbl = lbl if lbl in MODES[t] else \"other\"\n                if lbl in e[\"banned\"]:\n                    continue\n                vals.append(0.0 if lbl == \"other\" else math.log(0.93 * q[lbl] + 0.05 / len(allowed))\n                            - math.log(0.98 / len(allowed)))\n            ds.append(np.mean(vals) if vals else float(\"nan\"))\n        des.append(np.nanmean(ds))\n        qbar = {j: np.mean([q[j] for q in qs]) for j in allowed}\n        if e[\"clear_mode\"]:\n            ncl += 1\n            hits += int(max(qbar, key=qbar.get) == e[\"realised_mode\"])\n    out[\"checks\"][\"gate_b_absdiff\"] = abs(np.mean(des) - gate[\"b\"][\"mean_d_e\"])\n    out[\"checks\"][\"gate_c_hits_equal\"] = float(hits == gate[\"c\"][\"hits\"] and ncl == gate[\"c\"][\"n_clear\"])\n    # C1 point SHARE (primary reference) and AIM\n    c1 = json.loads((ROOT / \"results\" / \"c1.json\").read_text())[\"primary_neutral\"]\n    assoc = json.loads((ROOT / \"results\" / \"association.json\").read_text())[\"profiles\"]\n    pooled = {\"Vu\": 0.0, \"Vs\": 0.0}\n    aims = []\n    placebo_rows = []\n    for t in MODES:\n        js = meas[t]\n        nonce = np.mean([[L(t, f\"ban|nonce|{w}\", j) - Lmix(t, f\"neutral|nonce|{w}|A\", f\"neutral|nonce|{w}|B\", j)\n                          for j in js] for w in WS], axis=0)\n        base = np.array([L(t, \"base0\", j) for j in js])\n        rand = np.mean([[L(t, f\"edit|{d}\", j) for j in js] for d in RAND], axis=0) - base\n        nov = np.array([L(t, \"novelty\", j) for j in js]) - base\n        u = (nonce + rand + nov) / 3\n        Vu = Vs = 0.0\n        for B in MODES[t]:\n            Sv = [j for j in js if j != B]\n            if len(Sv) < 3:\n                continue\n            idx = [js.index(j) for j in Sv]\n            reps, r_h = [], []\n            for w in WS:\n                for h, ns in ((\"h1\", \"A\"), (\"h2\", \"B\")):\n                    g = np.array([L(t, f\"ban|{B}|{w}\", j, h) - L(t, f\"neutral|{B}|{w}|{ns}\", j, h) for j in Sv])\n                    reps.append(g - g.mean())\n                    r_h.append(h)\n            C = np.array(reps)\n            G = C.mean(0)\n            ub = u[idx] - u[idx].mean()\n            uh = ub / np.linalg.norm(ub)\n            a = G @ uh\n            s = G - a * uh\n            tot = C.var(axis=0, ddof=1).sum() / len(C)\n            sig = tot / (len(Sv) - 1)\n            Vu += a * a - sig\n            Vs += s @ s - (len(Sv) - 2) * sig\n            res_h = {}\n            for hn in (\"h1\", \"h2\"):\n                Gh = np.array([c for c, hh in zip(C, r_h) if hh == hn]).mean(0)\n                res_h[hn] = Gh - (Gh @ uh) * uh\n            pv = {}\n            for hn, ns in ((\"h1\", \"A\"), (\"h2\", \"B\")):\n                pv[hn] = np.mean([[L(t, f\"mention|{B}|{w}\", j, hn) - L(t, f\"neutral|{B}|{w}|{ns}\", j, hn) for j in Sv]\n                                  for w in WS], axis=0)\n            rho = np.nanmean([spearmanr(res_h[\"h1\"], pv[\"h2\"]).correlation, spearmanr(res_h[\"h2\"], pv[\"h1\"]).correlation])\n            aims.append(rho)\n            placebo_rows.append((res_h, pv))\n        share = Vs / (Vs + Vu)\n        out[\"checks\"][f\"share_{t}_absdiff\"] = abs(share - c1[\"tasks\"][t][\"share_point\"][\"SHARE\"])\n        pooled[\"Vu\"] += Vu\n        pooled[\"Vs\"] += Vs\n    out[\"checks\"][\"share_pooled_absdiff\"] = abs(pooled[\"Vs\"] / (pooled[\"Vs\"] + pooled[\"Vu\"])\n                                                - c1[\"pooled\"][\"share_point\"][\"SHARE\"])\n    aim_c1 = json.loads((ROOT / \"results\" / \"c1.json\").read_text())[\"primary_neutral\"].get(\"aim_prime_veto\", {})\n    out[\"checks\"][\"aim_absdiff\"] = abs(float(np.nanmean(aims)) - aim_c1.get(\"aim\", float(\"nan\")))\n    # placebo: PV profiles shuffled across rows (template/row labels permuted) -> aim near its null\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(5)\n    pl = []\n    for _ in range(200):\n        vals = []\n        for res_h, pv in placebo_rows:\n            perm = rng.permutation(len(pv[\"h1\"]))\n            vals.append(np.nanmean([spearmanr(res_h[\"h1\"], pv[\"h2\"][perm]).correlation,\n                                    spearmanr(res_h[\"h2\"], pv[\"h1\"][perm]).correlation]))\n        pl.append(np.nanmean(vals))\n    out[\"placebo_aim\"] = {\"mean\": float(np.mean(pl)), \"sd\": float(np.std(pl)), \"observed\": float(np.nanmean(aims))}\n    tol = 1e-9\n    out[\"all_match\"] = bool(all((v < tol) if \"absdiff\" in k else (v == 1.0) for k, v in out[\"checks\"].items()))\n    (ROOT / \"results\" / \"independent_rederivation.json\").write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=1, default=float))\n    print(json.dumps(out, indent=1, default=float))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()", "stderr": "", "interrupted": false, "isImage": false, "noOutputExpected": false}

### [55] TOOL CALL — Write · 2026-10-26 09:19:21 UTC

```
File: /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_jSassd-YWLVC/4_gen_paper_repo/_4_gen_demo_art/notebook_workspaces/iter_2/art_6zBfl3kDjeiP/build_mini_demo_data.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build mini_demo_data.json for code_demo.ipynb from the FRLP ban-screen artifact (experiment 7).

The artifact's GPU stages (vLLM generation, Qwen3-8B teacher-forced scoring) produced one parquet file per scoring
unit (condition x program set). This script keeps the first M forced programs per (task, algorithm) by their
pre-registered selection rank (M = 9 -> 54 + 45 = 99 programs; every algorithm-half stays non-empty) and stores, for
each program, its teacher-forced log-likelihoods [total, body, cum256] under every scored condition. It adds the small
side tables the CPU analysis stages read (yields, gate events, association profiles, prompt segments) and the
full-run headline numbers (M = 20, 211 programs) so the notebook can compare its demo-scale numbers with them.

Usage (the artifact folder is the parent of this demo folder in the published repository):
    uv run --no-project --python 3.12 --with pandas==2.2.2 --with pyarrow==18.1.0 --with loguru==0.7.3 \
        python build_mini_demo_data.py --src ..
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd
from loguru import logger

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
SCORE_COLS = ["total", "body", "cum256"]          # the three columns frlp.py reads (SCORE_COLS there)
PROG_FIELDS = ["prog_id", "task", "j", "family", "rank", "m_j", "half", "paraphrase_id", "passed", "label",
               "labeller_source", "scrub_mode", "n_output_tokens", "code_raw", "code_scrubbed"]
EVENT_FIELDS = ["event_id", "task", "condition", "banned", "allowed", "n_samples", "distribution_attempted",
                "distribution_allowed", "n_banned_label_samples", "realised_mode", "realised_top_share", "clear_mode"]


def read_jsonl(p: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [json.loads(line) for line in p.read_text().splitlines() if line.strip()]


def read_json(p: Path) -> Any:
    return json.loads(p.read_text())


def load_scores(src: Path, keep: set[str]) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[float]]]:
    """prog_id -> {"<variant>|<cond_id>": [total, body, cum256]}; first occurrence wins (as pivot 'first')."""
    units = read_jsonl(src / "scores" / "units_done.jsonl")
    seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
    out: dict[str, dict[str, list[float]]] = {}
    n_units = 0
    for u in units:
        if (u["task"], u["uid"]) in seen:   # uids are unique within a task only (as in frlp.load_scores)
            continue
        seen.add((u["task"], u["uid"]))
        p = src / "scores" / "cells" / u["task"] / f"{u['uid']}.parquet"
        if not p.exists():
            logger.warning(f"missing score file {p.name}")
            continue
        df = pd.read_parquet(p, columns=["prog_id"] + SCORE_COLS)
        key = u["variant"] + "|" + u["cond_id"]
        n_units += 1
        for row in df.itertuples(index=False):
            if row.prog_id not in keep:
                continue
            d = out.setdefault(row.prog_id, {})
            if key not in d:
                d[key] = [round(float(getattr(row, c)), 6) for c in SCORE_COLS]
    logger.info(f"read {n_units} scoring units; {sum(len(v) for v in out.values())} scores for {len(out)} programs")
    return out


def compact_event(e: dict[str, Any], n_para: int | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    ev = {k: e[k] for k in EVENT_FIELDS if k in e}
    paras = e["paraphrases"] if n_para is None else e["paraphrases"][:n_para]
    ev["paraphrases"] = [{"paraphrase_id": p["paraphrase_id"], "labels": p["labels"],
                          **({"prompt": p["prompt"]} if i == 0 else {})} for i, p in enumerate(paras)]
    return ev


def full_run_reference(src: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Headline numbers of the full run (M = 20 programs per algorithm, 2,000 bootstrap reps, 200,000-draw nulls)."""
    g = read_json(src / "results" / "gate.json")
    c = read_json(src / "results" / "c1.json")
    s = read_json(src / "results" / "sensitivity.json")
    r = read_json(src / "results" / "independent_rederivation.json")
    tp = read_json(src / "logs" / "throughput.json")

    def gate_summary(x: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"mean_S": x["a"]["mean_S"], "mean_m_default": x["a"]["mean_m_default"], "a_pass": x["a"]["pass"],
                "mean_d_e": x["b"]["mean_d_e"], "b_pass": x["b"]["pass"], "argmax_rate": x["c"]["rate"],
                "c_pass": x["c"]["pass"], "pass": x["pass"]}

    def c1_summary(x: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        out = {"pooled": {"SHARE": x["pooled"]["share_point"]["SHARE"],
                          "SHARE_ci": x["pooled"]["bootstrap"]["SHARE_ci"], "branch": x["pooled"]["branch"]}}
        for t in ("t1", "t2"):
            out[t] = {"SHARE": x["tasks"][t]["share_point"]["SHARE"],
                      "SHARE_ci": x["tasks"][t]["bootstrap"]["SHARE_ci"], "branch": x["tasks"][t]["branch"]}
        return out

    return {
        "M_programs_per_algorithm": tp["M"], "gate_paraphrases": tp["gate_paraphrases"], "n_boot": 2000,
        "n_perm": 200000, "sens_boot": 400,
        "gate_verdict": g["verdict"], "gate_primary": {**gate_summary(g["primary"]),
                                                      "pooled_spearman": g["primary"]["pooled_spearman_implied_vs_realised"],
                                                      "ess_cells": g["primary"]["ess_cells"]},
        "gate_sensitivity": {v: gate_summary(x) for v, x in g["sensitivity"].items()},
        "gate_events": {e["event_id"]: {"argmax": e["argmax"], "hit": e["hit"], "d_e": e["d_e"]}
                        for e in g["primary"]["events"]},
        "c1": {k: c1_summary(c[k]) for k in ("primary_neutral", "reference_mention", "ess_weighted")},
        "aim_prime_veto": c["primary_neutral"]["aim_prime_veto"],
        "aim_association": c["primary_neutral"]["aim_association"],
        "robust_pooled": c["robust_pooled"],
        "sensitivity": {k: {"SHARE": v["pooled_share_point"]["SHARE"], "SHARE_ci": v["pooled_bootstrap"]["SHARE_ci"],
                            "branch": v["pooled_branch"]} for k, v in s.items() if "error" not in v},
        "independent_rederivation_all_match": r["all_match"],
    }


@logger.catch(reraise=True)
def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--src", type=Path, default=HERE.parent, help="artifact folder (contains method.py, scores/)")
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=HERE / "mini_demo_data.json")
    ap.add_argument("--m", type=int, default=9, help="programs kept per (task, algorithm), by selection rank")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    logger.remove()
    logger.add(sys.stdout, level="INFO", format="{time:HH:mm:ss}|{level:<7}|{message}")
    (HERE / "logs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    logger.add(str(HERE / "logs" / "build_mini_demo_data.log"), rotation="30 MB", level="DEBUG")
    src = a.src.resolve()
    if not (src / "scores" / "units_done.jsonl").exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{src} is not the FRLP artifact folder (scores/units_done.jsonl missing)")

    sel = read_jsonl(src / "data" / "forced_selected.jsonl")
    progs = [r for r in sel if r["rank"] < min(r["m_j"], a.m)]           # file order kept (frlp/c1 rely on it)
    keep = {r["prog_id"] for r in progs}
    logger.info(f"kept {len(progs)} of {len(sel)} selected programs (rank < {a.m})")
    scores = load_scores(src, keep)
    examples = [{**{k: r[k] for k in PROG_FIELDS}, "scores": scores.get(r["prog_id"], {})} for r in progs]

    ev = read_json(src / "results" / "gate_events.json")
    pv = read_json(src / "prompts" / "prompts_v2.json")
    tp = read_json(src / "logs" / "throughput.json")
    meta = {
        "description": "FRLP ban screen (Qwen/Qwen3-8B, revision b968826d): forced programs with their teacher-forced "
                       "log-likelihoods under every scored prompt condition; demo subset of the first "
                       f"{a.m} programs per (task, algorithm) by selection rank.",
        "model": "Qwen/Qwen3-8B", "model_revision": "b968826d9c46dd6066d109eabc6255188de91218",
        "score_columns": SCORE_COLS,
        "score_key_format": "<variant>|<cond_id>; variant in {primary, unscrubbed, edit}",
        "M_demo": a.m, "M_full_run": tp["M"], "gate_paraphrases": tp["gate_paraphrases"],
        "yields": read_json(src / "results" / "yields.json")["yields"],
        "gate_events": {"events": [compact_event(e, None) for e in ev["events"]],
                        "succession_step2_descriptive": [compact_event(e, None)
                                                         for e in ev["succession_step2_descriptive"]]},
        "association_profiles": read_json(src / "results" / "association.json")["profiles"],
        "prompts": {"rules": pv["rules"], "nouns": pv["nouns"],
                    "task_spec": {t: pv["conditions"][f"{t}|base0"]["prompt"] for t in ("t1", "t2")},
                    "segments": {k: v.get("segment") for k, v in pv["conditions"].items()}},
        "full_run_reference": full_run_reference(src),
    }
    out = {"metadata": meta, "datasets": [{"dataset": "frlp_forced_programs", "examples": examples}]}
    a.out.write_text(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")))
    logger.info(f"wrote {a.out.name}: {len(examples)} examples, {a.out.stat().st_size / 1e6:.2f} MB")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

load_scores(src: Path, keep: set[str]) -> dict[str, dict[str, list[float]]]:\n    \"\"\"prog_id -> {\"<variant>|<cond_id>\": [total, body, cum256]}; first occurrence wins (as pivot 'first').\"\"\"\n (u[\"task\"], u[\"uid\"]) in seen:   # uids are unique within a task only (as in frlp.load_scores)\n            continue\n        seen.add((u[\"task\"], u[\"uid\"]))\n        p = src / \"scores\" / \"cells\" / u[\"task\"] / f\"{u['uid']}.parquet\"\n        if not p.exists():\n            logger.warning(f\"missing score file {p.name}\")\n            continue\n        df = pd.read_parquet(p, columns=[\"prog_id\"] + SCORE_COLS)\n        key = u[\"variant\"] + \"|\" + u[\"cond_id\"]\n        n_units += 1\n        for row in df.itertuples(index=False):\n            if row.prog_id not in keep:\n                continue\n            d = out.setdefault(row.prog_id, {})\n            if key not in d:\n                d[key] = [round(float(getattr(row, c)), 6) for c in SCORE_COLS]\n File: /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_jSassd-YWLVC/4_gen_paper_repo/_4_gen_demo_art/notebook_workspaces/iter_2/art_6zBfl3kDjeiP/code_demo.ipynb

{
 "cells": [
  {
   "cell_type": "markdown",
   "id": "title",
   "metadata": {},
   "source": [
    "# Do algorithm bans pick a substitute or a ladder? FRLP screen on Qwen3-8B (demo)\n",
    "\n",
    "When a prompt says *\"Do not use breadth-first search (BFS) for this task\"*, the model's probability mass leaves BFS. Where does it go? Two accounts compete:\n",
    "\n",
    "* **X-specific substitution**: the ban picks particular substitutes (banning BFS lifts DFS more than anything else), perhaps the ones the model *associates* with the banned name.\n",
    "* **Generic ladder**: the mass moves along a fixed profile that **any** perturbation of the prompt produces: a nonsense ban (*\"the Zorblax method\"*), matched-norm random edits of the prompt's hidden states, or a *\"write a novel algorithm\"* instruction.\n",
    "\n",
    "The artifact tests this claim (C1) on **Qwen/Qwen3-8B** (thinking off) and two path-search tasks: **T1** grid path with 6 algorithms (BFS, DFS, bidirectional BFS, IDDFS, A*, unit-weight Dijkstra), and **T2** weighted shortest path with 5 (Dijkstra, Bellman-Ford/SPFA, bidirectional Dijkstra, Floyd-Warshall, Dial).\n",
    "\n",
    "**Instrument: forced-ratio latent probabilities (FRLP).** Sampling cannot measure algorithms the model almost never writes. Instead, programs of every algorithm $j$ are *forced* (generated under 10 paraphrases such as *\"Implement $j$ for this task\"*), labelled by running them, scrubbed of algorithm names, and then **teacher-forced** under every prompt condition $c$. Importance sampling turns these log-likelihoods into an estimate of $\\log P(\\text{model writes } j \\mid c)$:\n",
    "\n",
    "$$L_j(c) = \\log y_j + \\log \\frac{1}{|P|} \\sum_{m \\in P} \\exp\\big[\\ell(p_m \\mid c) - \\ell_q(p_m)\\big], \\qquad \\ell_q(p) = \\log \\frac{1}{10} \\sum_{k=1}^{10} P(p \\mid F_j^k),$$\n",
    "\n",
    "where $y_j$ is the share of forced attempts that really implement $j$ (yield correction).\n",
    "\n",
    "**What this notebook runs.** `method.py` orchestrates 13 stages. The GPU stages cannot run in a short demo: vLLM generated 1,170 forced programs, and teacher-forced scoring of 684 condition x program-set units took about 3.3 GPU-hours on an L4. Their output is loaded from `mini_demo_data.json` instead: **99 of the 211 scored programs** (the first 9 per algorithm by their pre-registered selection rank), each with its log-likelihood under every scored condition. The CPU stages then run with the original code:\n",
    "\n",
    "| section | original file | what it computes |\n",
    "|---|---|---|\n",
    "| 4 | `src/frlp.py`, `src/analysis_misc.py` | FRLP cells, mixture-identity check, effective sample sizes (ESS) |\n",
    "| 5 | `src/gate.py` | FRLP validity gate against 19 events sampled in iteration 1 |\n",
    "| 6 | `src/c1.py` | C1 share test (ladder vs X-specific), AIM tests, sensitivity, shams, two-step bans |\n",
    "| 7 | `audit/rederive.py` | independent re-derivation of the headline numbers |\n",
    "| 8 | `src/outputs.py` | tables and figures, next to the full run (M = 20 programs per algorithm) |\n",
    "\n",
    "**Full-run results, for orientation.**\n",
    "\n",
    "* The gate fails on calibration (a): the scrubbed programs give DFS too much mass under the T1 baseline. The *unscrubbed* programs pass all three gate criteria, so the failure is a name-scrubbing artifact.\n",
    "* The pooled X-specific SHARE is 0.55 [0.25, 0.67] with neutral twins, so the branch is UNDETERMINED.\n",
    "* The residuals are not aimed at the prime-and-veto profile (AIM -0.10, p = 0.80), and the generic profiles do not form a coherent ladder.\n",
    "\n",
    "With 9 instead of 20 programs per algorithm the demo numbers are noisier, but they come from the same pipeline."
   ]
  }
 ],

}

FRLP screen on Qwen3-8B (demo)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"When a prompt says *\\\"Do not use breadth-first search (BFS) for this task\\\"*, the model's probability mass leaves BFS. Where does it go? Two accounts compete:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* **X-specific substitution**: the ban picks particular substitutes (banning BFS lifts DFS more than anything else), perhaps the ones the model *associates* with the banned name.\\n\",\n    \"* **Generic ladder**: the mass moves along a fixed profile that **any** perturbation of the prompt produces: a nonsense ban (*\\\"the Zorblax method\\\"*), matched-norm random edits of the prompt's hidden states, or a *\\\"write a novel algorithm\\\"* instruction.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"The artifact tests this claim (C1) on **Qwen/Qwen3-8B** (thinking off) and two path-search tasks: **T1** grid path with 6 algorithms (BFS, DFS, bidirectional BFS, IDDFS, A*, unit-weight Dijkstra), and **T2** weighted shortest path with 5 (Dijkstra, Bellman-Ford/SPFA, bidirectional Dijkstra, Floyd-Warshall, Dial).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Instrument: forced-ratio latent probabilities (FRLP).** Sampling cannot measure algorithms the model almost never writes. Instead, programs of every algorithm $j$ are *forced* (generated under 10 paraphrases such as *\\\"Implement $j$ for this task\\\"*), labelled by running them, scrubbed of algorithm names, and then **teacher-forced** under every prompt condition $c$. Importance sampling turns these log-likelihoods into an estimate of $\\\\log P(\\\\text{model writes } j \\\\mid c)$:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"$$L_j(c) = \\\\log y_j + \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{|P|} \\\\sum_{m \\\\in P} \\\\exp\\\\big[\\\\ell(p_m \\\\mid c) - \\\\ell_q(p_m)\\\\big], \\\\qquad \\\\ell_q(p) = \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{10} \\\\sum_{k=1}^{10} P(p \\\\mid F_j^k),$$\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"where $y_j$ is the share of forced attempts that really implement $j$ (yield correction).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**What this notebook runs.** `method.py` orchestrates 13 stages. The GPU stages cannot run in a short demo: vLLM generated 1,170 forced programs, and teacher-forced scoring of 684 condition x program-set units took about 3.3 GPU-hours on an L4. FRLP screen on Qwen3-8B (demo)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"When a prompt says *\\\"Do not use breadth-first search (BFS) for this task\\\"*, the model's probability mass leaves BFS. Where does it go? Two accounts compete:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* **X-specific substitution**: the ban picks particular substitutes (banning BFS lifts DFS more than anything else), perhaps the ones the model *associates* with the banned name.\\n\",\n    \"* **Generic ladder**: the mass moves along a fixed profile that **any** perturbation of the prompt produces: a nonsense ban (*\\\"the Zorblax method\\\"*), matched-norm random edits of the prompt's hidden states, or a *\\\"write a novel algorithm\\\"* instruction.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"The artifact tests this claim (C1) on **Qwen/Qwen3-8B** (thinking off) and two path-search tasks: **T1** grid path with 6 algorithms (BFS, DFS, bidirectional BFS, IDDFS, A*, unit-weight Dijkstra), and **T2** weighted shortest path with 5 (Dijkstra, Bellman-Ford/SPFA, bidirectional Dijkstra, Floyd-Warshall, Dial).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Instrument: forced-ratio latent probabilities (FRLP).** Sampling cannot measure algorithms the model almost never writes. Instead, programs of every algorithm $j$ are *forced* (generated under 10 paraphrases such as *\\\"Implement $j$ for this task\\\"*), labelled by running them, scrubbed of algorithm names, and then **teacher-forced** under every prompt condition $c$. Importance sampling turns these log-likelihoods into an estimate of $\\\\log P(\\\\text{model writes } j \\\\mid c)$:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"$$L_j(c) = \\\\log y_j + \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{|P|} \\\\sum_{m \\\\in P} \\\\exp\\\\big[\\\\ell(p_m \\\\mid c) - \\\\ell_q(p_m)\\\\big], \\\\qquad \\\\ell_q(p) = \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{10} \\\\sum_{k=1}^{10} P(p \\\\mid F_j^k),$$\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"where $y_j$ is the share of forced attempts that really implement $j$ (yield correction).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**What this notebook runs.** `method.py` orchestrates 13 stages. The GPU stages cannot run in a short demo: vLLM generated 1,170 forced programs, and teacher-forced scoring of 684 condition x program-set units took about 3.3 GPU-hours on an L4. Their output is loaded from `mini_demo_data.json` instead: **99 of the 211 scored programs** (the first 9 per algorithm by their pre-registered selection rank), each with its log-likelihood under every scored condition. The CPU stages then run with the original code:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"| section | original file | what it computes |\\n\",\n    \"|---|---|---|\\n\",\n    \"| 4 | `src/frlp.py`, `src/analysis_misc.py` | FRLP cells, mixture-identity check, effective sample sizes (ESS) |\\n\",\n    \"| 5 | `src/gate.py` | FRLP validity gate against 19 events sampled in iteration 1 |\\n\",\n    \"| 6 | `src/c1.py` | C1 share test (ladder vs X-specific), AIM tests, sensitivity, shams, two-step bans |\\n\",\n    \"| 7 | `audit/rederive.py` | independent re-derivation of the headline numbers |\\n\",\n    \"| 8 | `src/outputs.py` | tables and figures, next to the full run (M = 20 programs per algorithm) |\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Full-run results, for orientation.**\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* The gate fails on calibration (a): the scrubbed programs give DFS too much mass under the T1 baseline. The *unscrubbed* programs pass all three gate criteria, so the failure is a name-scrubbing artifact.\\n\",\n    \"* The pooled X-specific SHARE is 0.55 [0.25, 0.67] with neutral twins, so the branch is UNDETERMINED.\\n\",\n    \"* The residuals are not aimed at the prime-and-veto profile (AIM -0.10, p = 0.80), and the generic profiles do not form a coherent ladder.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"With 9 instead of 20 programs per algorithm the demo numbers are noisier, but they come from the same pipeline.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"6977089f\",\n   \"source\": \"import subprocess, sys\\ndef _pip(*a): subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, '-m', 'pip', 'install', '-q', *a])\\n\\n# loguru, rapidfuzz — NOT on Colab, always install (same pins as the artifact's pyproject.toml)\\n_pip('loguru==0.7.3', 'rapidfuzz==3.14.6')\\n\\n# numpy, pandas, scipy, matplotlib — pre-installed on Colab, install locally only (Colab's exact versions)\\nif 'google.colab' not in sys.modules:\\n    _pip('numpy==2.0.2', 'pandas==2.2.2', 'scipy==1.16.3', 'matplotlib==3.10.0')\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"outputs\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n  },\n  \"language_info\": {\n   \"name\": \"python\",\n   \"version\": \"3.12\"\n  }\n },\n \"nbformat\": 4,\n \"nbformat_minor\": 5\n}", "updated_file": "{\n \"cells\": [\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"id\": \"title\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"# Do algorithm bans pick a substitute or a ladder? FRLP screen on Qwen3-8B (demo)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"When a prompt says *\\\"Do not use breadth-first search (BFS) for this task\\\"*, the model's probability mass leaves BFS. Where does it go? Two accounts compete:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* **X-specific substitution**: the ban picks particular substitutes (banning BFS lifts DFS more than anything else), perhaps the ones the model *associates* with the banned name.\\n\",\n    \"* **Generic ladder**: the mass moves along a fixed profile that **any** perturbation of the prompt produces: a nonsense ban (*\\\"the Zorblax method\\\"*), matched-norm random edits of the prompt's hidden states, or a *\\\"write a novel algorithm\\\"* instruction.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"The artifact tests this claim (C1) on **Qwen/Qwen3-8B** (thinking off) and two path-search tasks: **T1** grid path with 6 algorithms (BFS, DFS, bidirectional BFS, IDDFS, A*, unit-weight Dijkstra), and **T2** weighted shortest path with 5 (Dijkstra, Bellman-Ford/SPFA, bidirectional Dijkstra, Floyd-Warshall, Dial).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Instrument: forced-ratio latent probabilities (FRLP).** Sampling cannot measure algorithms the model almost never writes. Instead, programs of every algorithm $j$ are *forced* (generated under 10 paraphrases such as *\\\"Implement $j$ for this task\\\"*), labelled by running them, scrubbed of algorithm names, and then **teacher-forced** under every prompt condition $c$. Importance sampling turns these log-likelihoods into an estimate of $\\\\log P(\\\\text{model writes } j \\\\mid c)$:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"$$L_j(c) = \\\\log y_j + \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{|P|} \\\\sum_{m \\\\in P} \\\\exp\\\\big[\\\\ell(p_m \\\\mid c) - \\\\ell_q(p_m)\\\\big], \\\\qquad \\\\ell_q(p) = \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{10} \\\\sum_{k=1}^{10} P(p \\\\mid F_j^k),$$\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"where $y_j$ is the share of forced attempts that really implement $j$ (yield correction).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**What this notebook runs.** `method.py` orchestrates 13 stages. The GPU stages cannot run in a short demo: vLLM generated 1,170 forced programs, and teacher-forced scoring of 684 condition x program-set units took about 3.3 GPU-hours on an L4. Their output is loaded from `mini_demo_data.json` instead: **99 of the 211 scored programs** (the first 9 per algorithm by their pre-registered selection rank), each with its log-likelihood under every scored condition. The CPU stages then run with the original code:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"| section | original file | what it computes |\\n\",\n    \"|---|---|---|\\n\",\n    \"| 4 | `src/frlp.py`, `src/analysis_misc.py` | FRLP cells, mixture-identity check, effective sample sizes (ESS) |\\n\",\n    \"| 5 | `src/gate.py` | FRLP validity gate against 19 events sampled in iteration 1 |\\n\",\n    \"| 6 | `src/c1.py` | C1 share test (ladder vs X-specific), AIM tests, sensitivity, shams, two-step bans |\\n\",\n    \"| 7 | `audit/rederive.py` | independent re-derivation of the headline numbers |\\n\",\n    \"| 8 | `src/outputs.py` | tables and figures, next to the full run (M = 20 programs per algorithm) |\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Full-run results, for orientation.**\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* The gate fails on calibration (a): the scrubbed programs give DFS too much mass under the T1 baseline. The *unscrubbed* programs pass all three gate criteria, so the failure is a name-scrubbing artifact.\\n\",\n    \"* The pooled X-specific SHARE is 0.55 [0.25, 0.67] with neutral twins, so the branch is UNDETERMINED.\\n\",\n    \"* The residuals are not aimed at the prime-and-veto profile (AIM -0.10, p = 0.80), and the generic profiles do not form a coherent ladder.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"With algorithm bans pick a substitute or a ladder? FRLP screen on Qwen3-8B (demo)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"When a prompt says *\\\"Do not use breadth-first search (BFS) for this task\\\"*, the model's probability mass leaves BFS. Where does it go? Two accounts compete:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* **X-specific substitution**: the ban picks particular substitutes (banning BFS lifts DFS more than anything else), perhaps the ones the model *associates* with the banned name.\\n\",\n    \"* **Generic ladder**: the mass moves along a fixed profile that **any** perturbation of the prompt produces: a nonsense ban (*\\\"the Zorblax method\\\"*), matched-norm random edits of the prompt's hidden states, or a *\\\"write a novel algorithm\\\"* instruction.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"The artifact tests this claim (C1) on **Qwen/Qwen3-8B** (thinking off) and two path-search tasks: **T1** grid path with 6 algorithms (BFS, DFS, bidirectional BFS, IDDFS, A*, unit-weight Dijkstra), and **T2** weighted shortest path with 5 (Dijkstra, Bellman-Ford/SPFA, bidirectional Dijkstra, Floyd-Warshall, Dial).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Instrument: forced-ratio latent probabilities (FRLP).** Sampling cannot measure algorithms the model almost never writes. Instead, programs of every algorithm $j$ are *forced* (generated under 10 paraphrases such as *\\\"Implement $j$ for this task\\\"*), labelled by running them, scrubbed of algorithm names, and then **teacher-forced** under every prompt condition $c$. Importance sampling turns these log-likelihoods into an estimate of $\\\\log P(\\\\text{model writes } j \\\\mid c)$:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"$$L_j(c) = \\\\log y_j + \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{|P|} \\\\sum_{m \\\\in P} \\\\exp\\\\big[\\\\ell(p_m \\\\mid c) - \\\\ell_q(p_m)\\\\big], \\\\qquad \\\\ell_q(p) = \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{10} \\\\sum_{k=1}^{10} P(p \\\\mid F_j^k),$$\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"where $y_j$ is the share of forced attempts that really implement $j$ (yield correction).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**What this notebook runs.** `method.py` orchestrates 13 stages. The GPU stages cannot run in a short demo: vLLM generated 1,170 forced programs, and teacher-forced scoring of 684 condition x program-set units took about 3.3 GPU-hours on an L4. Their output is loaded from `mini_demo_data.json` instead: **99 of the 211 scored programs** (the first 9 per algorithm by their pre-registered selection rank), each with its log-likelihood under every scored condition. The *unscrubbed* programs pass all three gate criteria, so the failure is a name-scrubbing artifact.\\n\",\n    \"* The pooled X-specific SHARE is 0.55 [0.25, 0.67] with neutral twins, so the branch is UNDETERMINED.\\n\",\n    \"* The residuals are not aimed at the prime-and-veto profile (AIM -0.10, p = 0.80), and the generic profiles do not form a coherent ladder.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"With 9 instead of 20 programs per algorithm the demo numbers are noisier, but they come from the same pipeline.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"6977089f\",\n   \"source\": FRLP screen on Qwen3-8B (demo)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"When a prompt says *\\\"Do not use breadth-first search (BFS) for this task\\\"*, the model's probability mass leaves BFS. Where does it go? Two accounts compete:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* **X-specific substitution**: the ban picks particular substitutes (banning BFS lifts DFS more than anything else), perhaps the ones the model *associates* with the banned name.\\n\",\n    \"* **Generic ladder**: the mass moves along a fixed profile that **any** perturbation of the prompt produces: a nonsense ban (*\\\"the Zorblax method\\\"*), matched-norm random edits of the prompt's hidden states, or a *\\\"write a novel algorithm\\\"* instruction.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"The artifact tests this claim (C1) on **Qwen/Qwen3-8B** (thinking off) and two path-search tasks: **T1** grid path with 6 algorithms (BFS, DFS, bidirectional BFS, IDDFS, A*, unit-weight Dijkstra), and **T2** weighted shortest path with 5 (Dijkstra, Bellman-Ford/SPFA, bidirectional Dijkstra, Floyd-Warshall, Dial).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Instrument: forced-ratio latent probabilities (FRLP).** Sampling cannot measure algorithms the model almost never writes. Instead, programs of every algorithm $j$ are *forced* (generated under 10 paraphrases such as *\\\"Implement $j$ for this task\\\"*), labelled by running them, scrubbed of algorithm names, and then **teacher-forced** under every prompt condition $c$. Importance sampling turns these log-likelihoods into an estimate of $\\\\log P(\\\\text{model writes } j \\\\mid c)$:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"$$L_j(c) = \\\\log y_j + \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{|P|} \\\\sum_{m \\\\in P} \\\\exp\\\\big[\\\\ell(p_m \\\\mid c) - \\\\ell_q(p_m)\\\\big], \\\\qquad \\\\ell_q(p) = \\\\log \\\\frac{1}{10} \\\\sum_{k=1}^{10} P(p \\\\mid F_j^k),$$\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"where $y_j$ is the share of forced attempts that really implement $j$ (yield correction).\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**What this notebook runs.** `method.py` orchestrates 13 stages. The GPU stages cannot run in a short demo: vLLM generated 1,170 forced programs, and teacher-forced scoring of 684 condition x program-set units took about 3.3 GPU-hours on an L4. Their output is loaded from `mini_demo_data.json` instead: **99 of the 211 scored programs** (the first 9 per algorithm by their pre-registered selection rank), each with its log-likelihood under every scored condition. The CPU stages then run with the original code:\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"| section | original file | what it computes |\\n\",\n    \"|---|---|---|\\n\",\n    \"| 4 | `src/frlp.py`, `src/analysis_misc.py` | FRLP cells, mixture-identity check, effective sample sizes (ESS) |\\n\",\n    \"| 5 | `src/gate.py` | FRLP validity gate against 19 events sampled in iteration 1 |\\n\",\n    \"| 6 | `src/c1.py` | C1 share test (ladder vs X-specific), AIM tests, sensitivity, shams, two-step bans |\\n\",\n    \"| 7 | `audit/rederive.py` | independent re-derivation of the headline numbers |\\n\",\n    \"| 8 | `src/outputs.py` | tables and figures, next to the full run (M = 20 programs per algorithm) |\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"**Full-run results, for orientation.**\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"* The gate fails on calibration (a): the scrubbed programs give DFS too much mass under the T1 baseline. The *unscrubbed* programs pass all three gate criteria, so the failure is a name-scrubbing artifact.\\n\",\n    \"* The pooled X-specific SHARE is 0.55 [0.25, 0.67] with neutral twins, so the branch is UNDETERMINED.\\n\",\n    \"* The residuals are not aimed at the prime-and-veto profile (AIM -0.10, p = 0.80), and the generic profiles do not form a coherent ladder.\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"With 9 instead of 20 programs per algorithm the demo numbers are noisier, but they come from the same pipeline.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"id\": \"6977089f\",\n   \"source\": Left out are the file paths, the I/O and hash-chain helpers, and the generation-only helpers; `N_BOOT` and `N_PERM` come from the configuration cell.\n\nHow the prompt conditions are built:\n\n* A **ban** prepends a sentence such as *"Do not use {X} for this task."* to the task statement.\n* Its **mention twin** says *"You could use {X} for this task."*\n* Its **neutral twin** bans a frequent noun instead (*"Do not use man for this task. . . . . . ."*), padded with `\' .\'` so that it has exactly as many tokens as the ban.\n\nEach of the five templates W1–W5 has all three forms.'}

The constants below are copied verbatim. Left out are the file paths, the I/O and hash-chain helpers, and the generation-only helpers; `N_BOOT` and `N_PERM` come from the configuration cell.\\n\\nHow the prompt conditions are built:\\n\\n* A **ban** prepends a sentence such as *\\\"Do not use {X} for this task.\\\"* to the task statement.\\n* Its **mention twin** says *\\\"You could use {X} for this task.\\\"*\\n* Its **neutral twin** bans a frequent noun instead (*\\\"Do not use man for this task. . . . . . .\\\"*), padded with `' .'` so that it has exactly as many tokens as the ban.\\n\\nEach of the five templates W1–W5 has all three forms.\",\n   \"metadata\": {}\n  }\n ],\n \"metadata\": {\n  \"kernelspec\": {\n   \"display_name\": \"Python 3\",\n   \"language\": \"python\",\n   \"name\": \"python3\"\n  },\n  \"language_info\": {\n   \"name\": \"python\",\n   \"version\": \"3.12\"\n  }\n },\n \"nbformat\": 4,\n \"nbformat_minor\": 5\n}"}

FRLP cells (`src/frlp.py`, step 4)\n\nTake task $t$, condition $c$, algorithm $j$ and a set $P$ of forced $j$-programs. Each program gets a log importance weight $lw_m = \\ell(p_m \\mid c) - \\ell_q(p_m)$, where $\\ell_q$ is the balance-heuristic mixture over the algorithm's 10 forced paraphrases. Then:\n\n* $L_j(c) = \\log y_j + \\operatorname{lse}_m(lw_m) - \\log |P|$ is the yield-corrected estimate of $\\log P(j \\mid c)$;\n* $\\mathrm{ESS} = \\exp\\big(2\\,\\operatorname{lse}(lw) - \\operatorname{lse}(2\\,lw)\\big)$ is the effective number of programs behind a cell.\n\n`VARIANTS` are the robustness variants. Each one changes a single ingredient:\n\n* the score column (`body`, `cum256`);\n* the programs used (only test-passing ones);\n* the proposal (the generating paraphrase alone, `single`);\n* the scrubbing (unscrubbed programs, with their own denominators).\n\n`CellArrays` recomputes every cell from per-program multiplicity weights, so the C1 bootstrap can resample programs within algorithm-halves without re-reading any scores.\n\nChanges from `frlp.py`:\n\n* `load_scores()` and `build_task_data()` read `mini_demo_data.json` instead of `scores/cells/*.parquet`, `data/forced_selected.jsonl` and `results/yields.json`.\n* `_m_scored()` returns `M_SCORED` instead of reading `logs/throughput.json`.\n* `write_cells()` returns its rows instead of writing `results/frlp_cells.jsonl`.\n* `coverage()` is left out, because it needs iteration 1's natural programs."}
```

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ fast arrays\nclass CellArrays:\n    """Vectorised cells for the bootstrap: for each condition row c and algorithm j, L over h1, h2 and all,\n    recomputed from per-program multiplicity weights W (W = 1 is the point estimate)."""\n\n    def __init__(self, td: TaskData, conds: list[str], vname: str = "primary",\n                 virtual: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] | None = None) -> None:\n        self.td, self.vname = td, vname\n        self.conds = list(conds) + list((virtual or {}).keys())\n        self.cidx = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(self.conds)}\n        self.js = [j for j in td.measurable if td.pids(j, "all", vname)]  # e.g. correct_only drops j with no passes\n        pids = [p for j in self.js for p in td.pids(j, "all", vname)]\n        self.pids = pids\n        self.pj = np.array([self.js.index(td.progs.at[p, "j"]) for p in pids])\n        self.ph = np.array([0 if td.progs.at[p, "half"] == "h1" else 1 for p in pids])\n        lw = np.full((len(self.conds), len(pids)), np.nan)\n        for i, c in enumerate(conds):\n            if td.has(c, vname):\n                lw[i] = td.lw(c, vname).reindex(pids).to_numpy(dtype=float)\n        for name, (ca, cb) in (virtual or {}).items():\n            i = self.cidx[name]\n            a = td.lw(ca, vname).reindex(pids).to_numpy(dtype=float) if td.has(ca, vname) else np.full(len(pids), np.nan)\n            b = td.lw(cb, vname).reindex(pids).to_numpy(dtype=float) if td.has(cb, vname) else np.full(len(pids), np.nan)\n            lw[i] = np.where(self.ph == 0, a, b)\n        self.lw = lw\n        self.valid = np.isfinite(lw)\n        shift = np.zeros((len(self.conds), len(self.js)))\n        for k in range(len(self.js)):\n            m = self.pj == k\n            if not m.any():\n                continue\n            sub = np.where(self.valid[:, m], lw[:, m], -np.inf)\n            mx = sub.max(axis=1)\n            shift[:, k] = np.where(np.isfinite(mx), mx, 0.0)\n        self.shift = shift\n        self.E = np.where(self.valid, np.exp(np.where(self.valid, lw, 0.0) - shift[:, self.pj]), 0.0)\n        self.E2 = self.E ** 2\n        y = td.yields_pass if VARIANTS[vname][2] == "passed" else td.yields\n        self.logy = np.log(np.maximum(np.array([y[j] for j in self.js]), 1e-12))\n\n    def cells(self, W: np.ndarray | None = None, with_ess: bool = False):\n        """Returns L[h] arrays (n_cond x K) for h in (\'h1\',\'h2\',\'all\'); NaN where not scored."""\n        if W is None:\n            W = np.ones(len(self.pids))\n        out, ess = {}, {}\n        for hname, hmask in (("h1", self.ph == 0), ("h2", self.ph == 1), ("all", np.ones(len(self.pids), bool))):\n            L = np.full((len(self.conds), len(self.js)), np.nan)\n            Es = np.full((len(self.conds), len(self.js)), np.nan)\n            for k in range(len(self.js)):\n                m = (self.pj == k) & hmask\n                if not m.any():\n                    continue\n                Wm = W[m]\n                Vm = self.valid[:, m]\n                n = Vm.astype(float) @ Wm\n                s = self.E[:, m] @ Wm\n                ok = (n > 0) & (s > 0)\n                L[ok, k] = self.logy[k] + self.shift[ok, k] + np.log(s[ok]) - np.log(n[ok])\n                if with_ess:\n                    s2 = self.E2[:, m] @ Wm\n                    Es[ok, k] = s[ok] ** 2 / s2[ok]\n            out[hname] = L\n            ess[hname] = Es\n        return (out, ess) if with_ess else out\n\n    def boot_weights(self, rng: np.random.Generator) -> np.ndarray:\n        W = np.zeros(len(self.pids))\n        for k in range(len(self.js)):\n            for h in (0, 1):\n                idx = np.where((self.pj == k) & (self.ph == h))[0]\n                if len(idx):\n                    W[idx] = rng.multinomial(len(idx), np.full(len(idx), 1 / len(idx)))\n        return W'}

There are 19 events: baselines, bans, mentions and a list-all prompt, with 30 labelled samples per paraphrase. The definitions were hash-committed before any score was read:\n\n* **(a) calibration** on the baseline paraphrases. $S = \\sum_j e^{L_j}$ must lie in [0.3, 3], and the default algorithm\'s share $m = e^{L_\\text{default}}/S$ must be at least 0.9. The implied argmax must equal the realised mode in at least 80% of clear-mode events.\n\nThe gate is re-run for every variant (`sensitivity`), including the unscrubbed programs. `succession_descriptive()` compares the FRLP landing after a two-step ban with iteration 1\'s samples.\n\nChanges from `gate.py`: `results/gate_events.json` is read from the data, and `main()`\'s output file becomes the variable `gate_out`.'}

ds.append(math.log(qp) - math.log(ua))\n            per_para.append({"paraphrase_id": p["paraphrase_id"], "d": float(np.mean(ds)) if ds else math.nan,\n                             "n_scored": res["c"] = {"hits": hits, "n_clear": n_clear, "rate": hits / max(1, n_clear),\n                "pass": bool(n_clear > 0 and hits / n_clear >= GATE_C_MIN)}\n    res["pass"] = bool(res["a"]["pass"] and / tot)\n        out[e["event_id"]] = {\n            "implied_landing": {j: float(np.mean([q[j] for q in qs])) for j in allowed} if qs else None,\n            "implied_banned_mass": float(np.mean(banned_mass)) if banned_mass else None,\n            "exp1_realised": e["distribution_attempted"], "exp1_banned_share":\n                e["n_banned_label_samples"] / max(1, e["n_samples"])}\n    return out'}

{'notebook_path': '/ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_jSassd-YWLVC/4_gen_paper_repo/_4_gen_demo_art/notebook_workspaces/iter_2/art_6zBfl3kDjeiP/code_demo.ipynb', 'cell_id': '9b0631a5', 'edit_mode': 'insert', 'cell_type': 'markdown', 'new_source': '## 5. C1 share test: ladder or X-specific? (`src/c1.py`, steps 6–7)\n\nFor each ban row $B$, the survivors $S_B$ are the measurable algorithms other than $B$.\n\n1. **Gain profile.** There are 10 replicates $r$ (5 templates × 2 program halves). Each gives $g_{Br}(j) = L^h_j(\\text{ban}_w B) - L^h_j(\\text{twin}_{w,h} B)$, centred over $j$, and $\\bar G_B$ is their mean. The twin is the neutral twin (primary reference) or the mention twin (reference 2).\n2. **Ladder profile $u$.** The mean of three *generic* perturbation profiles:\n   * NONCE: ban "the Zorblax method" vs its neutral twin;\n   * RAND: 6 matched-norm random edits of the prompt\'s hidden states vs base0;\n   * NOV: the novelty instruction vs base0.\n3. **Decomposition.** $a_B = \\bar G_B \\cdot \\hat u_B$ is the ladder part, and $s_B = \\bar G_B - a_B \\hat u_B$ is the X-specific residual.\n4. **Noise-corrected share.** With the replicate noise $\\sigma^2$ of each row:\n   * $V_u = \\sum_B (a_B^2 - \\sigma^2)$ and $V_s = \\sum_B \\big(\\lVert s_B\\rVert^2 - (|S_B|-2)\\,\\sigma^2\\big)$;\n   * **SHARE = $V_s / (V_s + V_u)$**;\n   * a bootstrap over templates, programs (within algorithm-halves) and random directions gives the CIs.\n5. **AIM.** Is $s_B$ (from one half) rank-correlated with the *prime-and-veto* profile (mention − neutral, from the other half and the other noun)? The null permutes the survivors within each row. The same test is run against the free-form association profile.\n6. **Branch.** The first rule that applies wins:\n   * PURE SUBTRACTION: the CI of $V_u + V_s$ reaches 0;\n   * SUBSTITUTION: SHARE\'s CI is above 0.5 and AIM is above its null 95th percentile;\n   * X-SPECIFIC-UNAIMED: SHARE\'s CI is above 0.5, but the residual is not aimed;\n   * LADDER: SHARE\'s CI is below 0.5 and the generic profiles agree (mean centred cosine ≥ 0.5);\n   * UNDETERMINED: none of the above.\n\nChanges from `c1.py`: `results/association.json` and `data/forced_selected.jsonl` are read from the data, the CLI arguments come from the configuration cell, and the result files become variables.'}
```

cx.v(f"neutral|nonce|{w}|AB" if half == "all" else\n                                                           f"neutral|nonce|{w}|{\'A\' if half == \'h1\' else \'B\'}", half)\n                        for w in templates], axis=0)\n    base = cx.v("base0", half)\n    rand = np.nanmean([cx.v(f"edit|{d}", half) - base for d in dirs], axis=0)\n    nov = cx.v("novelty", half) - base\n    out = {"NONCE": nonce, "RAND": rand, "NOV": nov}\n    if edit2:\n        out["RAND_ii"] = np.nanmean([cx.v(c, half) - base for c in edit2], axis=0)\n    return out\n\n\ndef ladder_u(fp: dict[str, np.ndarray], kind: str = "primary") -> np.ndarray:\n    if kind == "primary":\n        return np.mean([fp["NONCE"], fp["RAND"], fp["NOV"]], axis=0)\n    if kind == "with_ii":\n        return np.mean([fp[k] for k in ("NONCE", "RAND", "NOV", "RAND_ii") if k in fp], axis=0)\n    if kind == "unit_normalised":\n        vs = []\n        for k in ("NONCE", "RAND", "NOV"):\n            v = fp[k] - np.nanmean(fp[k])\n            n = np.linalg.norm(np.nan_to_num(v))\n            vs.append(v / n if n > 0 else v)\n        return np.mean(vs, axis=0)\n    if kind.startswith("only_"):\n        return fp[kind[5:]]\n    raise ValueError(kind)\n\n\ndef _wcentre(x: np.ndarray, v: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    return x - np.sum(v * x) / np.sum(v)\n\n\ndef row_stats(cx: Ctx, B: str, u: np.ndarray, templates: list[str], ref: str = "neutral",\n              ess_weight: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    js = cx.ca.js\n    S = [j for j in js if j != B]\n    if len(S) < 3:\n        return None\n    idx = np.array([js.index(j) for j in S])\n    reps, reps_h, ess_min = [], [], []\n    for w in templates:\n        for h, ns in (("h1", "A"), ("h2", "B")):\n            refc = f"neutral|{B}|{w}|{ns}" if ref == "neutral" else f"mention|{B}|{w}"\n            g = (cx.v(f"ban|{B}|{w}", h) - cx.v(refc, h))[idx]\n            reps.append(g)\n            reps_h.append(h)\n            if cx.ess is not None:\n                ess_min.append(np.fmin(cx.e(f"ban|{B}|{w}", h), cx.e(refc, h))[idx])\n    G = np.array(reps)\n    if not np.isfinite(G).all():\n        return None\n    if ess_weight and ess_min:\n        med = np.nanmedian(np.array(ess_min), axis=0)\n        vw = np.clip(med / ESS_FLAG, 0.0, 1.0)\n        vw = np.where(np.isfinite(vw), vw, 1.0)\n    else:\n        med = np.nanmedian(np.array(ess_min), axis=0) if ess_min else np.full(len(S), np.nan)\n        vw = np.ones(len(S))\n    C = np.array([_wcentre(g, vw) for g in G])\n    R = len(C)\n    Gbar = C.mean(0)\n    uB = _wcentre(u[idx], vw)\n    un = math.sqrt(np.sum(vw * uB * uB))\n    if not np.isfinite(un) or un == 0:\n        return None\n    uhat = uB / un\n    a = float(np.sum(vw * Gbar * uhat))\n    s = Gbar - a * uhat\n    ss_s = float(np.sum(vw * s * s))\n    tot = float(np.sum(vw * C.var(axis=0, ddof=1)) / R)\n    neff = float(np.sum(vw))\n    sigma2 = tot / (neff - 1)\n    N_u, N_s = sigma2, (neff - 2) * sigma2\n    proj = C @ (vw * uhat)\n    N_u_p = float(np.var(proj, ddof=1) / R)\n    N_s_p = tot - N_u_p\n    out = {"B": B, "survivors": S, "Gbar": Gbar, "uhat": uhat, "a": a, "s": s, "a2": a * a, "ss_s": ss_s,\n           "tot_noise": tot, "sigma2": sigma2, "N_u": N_u, "N_s": N_s, "N_u_proj": N_u_p, "N_s_proj": N_s_p,\n           "ss_G": float(np.sum(vw * Gbar * Gbar)), "R": R, "weights": vw, "median_ess": med,\n           "n_flagged_cells": int(np.sum(np.nan_to_num(med, nan=99.0) < ESS_FLAG))}\n    # half-specific residuals for the AIM (h1 replicates vs h2 replicates)\n    for hn in ("h1", "h2"):\n        Ch = np.array([c for c, hh in zip(C, reps_h) if hh == hn])\n        Gh = Ch.mean(0)\n        out[f"s_{hn}"] = Gh - float(np.sum(vw * Gh * uhat)) * uhat\n    return out\n\n\ndef share_from_rows(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], proj_noise: bool = False) -> dict[str, float]:\n    nu, ns = ("N_u_proj", "N_s_proj") if proj_noise else ("N_u", "N_s")\n    V_u = float(sum(r["a2"] - r[nu] for r in rows))\n    V_s = float(sum(r["ss_s"] - r[ns] for r in rows))\n    tot = V_u + V_s\n    F = float(sum(r["ss_G"] for r in rows) / max(1e-12, sum(r["tot_noise"] for r in rows)))\n    return {"V_u": V_u, "V_s": V_s, "V_tot": tot, "SHARE": V_s / tot if tot != 0 else math.nan, "F": F,\n            "n_rows": len(rows)}'}

rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n    total = np.zeros(n_perm)\n    n_rows = 0\n    for pairs in pairs_by_row:\n        n = len(pairs[0][0])\n        perms = np.argsort(rng.random((n_perm, n)), generic conditions, which were not scored on raw programs)."""\n    fp = family_profiles(cx_u or cx, WS if cx_u is not None else templates, dirs, edit2)\n    u = ladder_u(fp, u_kind)\n    rows = []\n    for B in MODES[td.task]:\n        r = row_stats(cx, B, u, templates, ref, ess_weight)\n        if r is not None:\n            rows.append(r)\n    return {"fp": fp, "u": u, "rows": rows, "share": share_from_rows(rows),\n            "share_proj": share_from_rows(rows, proj_noise=True)}\n\n\ndef ladder_exists(cx: Ctx, td: TaskData, templates: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    js = cx.ca.js\n    keep = [i for i, j in enumerate(js) if j != {"t1": "bfs", "t2": "dijkstra"}[td.task]]\n    base = cx.v("base0")\n    profs = {"NONCE": family_profiles(cx, templates, RAND_NAMES)["NONCE"]}\n    for d in RAND_NAMES:\n        profs[f"RAND_{d}"] = cx.v(f"edit|{d}") - base\n    profs["NOV"] = cx.v("novelty") - base\n    names = list(profs)\n    M = np.array([profs[n][keep] for n in names])\n    ok = np.isfinite(M).all(axis=1)\n    names = [n for n, o in zip(names, ok) if o]\n    M = M[ok]\n    cc, rc = [], []\n    for i in range(len(M)):\n        for k in range(i + 1, len(M)):\n            a, b = M[i], M[k]\n            ac, bc = a - a.mean(), b - b.mean()\n            cc.append(float(ac @ bc / (np.linalg.norm(ac) * np.linalg.norm(bc) + 1e-12)))\n            rc.append(float(a @ b / (np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b) + 1e-12)))\n    mat = {n: {m: float(np.corrcoef(M[i], M[k])[0, 1]) for k, m in enumerate(names)} for i, n in enumerate(names)}\n    sd = {n: float(np.std(M[i] - M[i].mean())) for i, n in enumerate(names)}\n    return {"profiles_over": [js[i] for i in keep], "n_profiles": len(names),\n            "mean_pairwise_centred_cosine": float(np.mean(cc)) if cc else math.nan,\n            "mean_pairwise_raw_cosine": float(np.mean(rc)) if rc else math.nan,\n            "exists": bool(cc and np.mean(cc) >= LADDER_COS_MIN), "matrix": mat, "between_survivor_sd": sd}\n\n\ndef branch(share_ci: tuple[float, float], vtot_ci: tuple[float, float], aim: float | None, aim_p95: float | None,\n           ladder: bool, n_rows: int = 1) -> str:\n    if n_rows == 0:\n        return "NOT RUN (no scored rows)"\n    if not np.isfinite(vtot_ci[0]) or vtot_ci[0] <= 0:\n        return "PURE SUBTRACTION"\n    if share_ci[0] > 0.5 and aim is not None and aim_p95 is not None and aim > aim_p95:\n        return "SUBSTITUTION"\n    if share_ci[0] > 0.5:\n        return "X-SPECIFIC-UNAIMED"\n    if share_ci[1] < 0.5 and ladder:\n        return "LADDER"\n    return "UNDETERMINED"'}
```

res["sham_observations_7_1"] = sham_obs(res, boot_comp)\n    return res\n\n\ndef _ci_block(comps: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    def ci(key):\n        v = np.array([c[key] for c in
